# GAIA mobile parity matrix

The source of truth for whether the phone app is at parity with GAIA's Agent UI. Owned by Parity; never committed. **Version 2, 2026-11-12.**

**Parity: 0 of 613 target rows done (0%).** 19 in progress, 589 missing, 5 different on purpose. 56 desktop-shell rows have no phone counterpart and aren't counted.

- **Reference:** main's Agent UI at `7a6e05c2f` (0.26.0). The upstream watch has read to `240b309b7`; nothing since then touches parity. Labels are quoted from main's `en.ts`, or from source where upstream hard-codes them.
- **Rich's installed gaia-desktop** is supporting evidence only. He ran 0.25.0 until 15:09 on 2026-11-12 and runs **0.25.4** since (Release). **Since** is the first release with the feature: `0.25` (v0.25.0), `0.25.4` (added by v0.25.4, so on his desktop now), or `0.26` (main only, so no shot of his app can show it). At least 457 of the 613 targets are on his 0.25.4 desktop. Up to 156 are main-only: 65 confirmed, and about 90 still marked `0.26` because their copy changed since 0.25.4 or they quote nothing to check (`.local/dev/parity-since-0254.mjs`).
- **Target:** the React Native (Expo) app (Rich, 2026-11-12). The web UI stays the mobile-web path.
- **Built from** the inventory in `parity-inventory/` (frozen at `7a6e05c2f`). Edit this file from now on; the upstream watch (`.local/dev/parity-upstream-watch.mjs`) says which rows to update.

## Status

| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `missing` | Not in the app. |
| `decide` | Rich decides whether the app offers it, or how (see Decisions). Counted as a target until he does. |
| `in progress` | A branch is building it; Notes names the branch. |
| `done` | Parity compared the app with the reference, screen by screen: the same labels (catalog keys, English and Arabic), order, every state in States, and the same API calls. Evidence names the shots. |
| `different` | Left out or changed on purpose, with the reason and who decided. A changed row stays a target for its agreed behavior; a left-out row drops out of the count. |
| `n/a` | A desktop-shell feature with no phone counterpart (Parity's call; the 56 rows were approved by Rich, via the Lead, 2026-11-12). |

**Evidence:** `main` is a fixture shot of main's UI (Design's `test:visual` scenes) and is preferred. `0.25.4` is a shot of Rich's installed 0.25.4 (`0.25` one of 0.25.0, taken before 15:09 on 2026-11-12); labels and screens may differ from main. `0.25.4` alone is English; `0.25.4 en+ar` and `0.25.4 ar` say the shots also, or only, show the row in Arabic. `code` means read from main's code with no shot yet. **PC-only:** the backend refuses the call over mobile access; the phone shows the same refusal (R1–R4, below) or the state read-only.

## Reference notes

Cross-cutting facts from the inventory: how Since and PC-only were judged, the refusal texts R1–R4, and what every phone request needs.

### From chat.md

Scope: 64 files under `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/components/` plus the guide sections named in the brief.

How to read this file

- Labels are quoted from `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/i18n/locales/en.ts` unless a row says "hard-coded". Many chat strings are still hard-coded English in the components (they are not in en.ts); those are quoted from the component and flagged. `{x}` is a template placeholder. `.one`/`.other` are plural pairs.
- **Since:** `en.ts` and `useT` do not exist at v0.25.0 (every string was hard-coded then), so Since was decided by whether the feature's code or English text exists in the v0.25.0 tree, not by key. A component file that is new in 0.26 may still hold a 0.25 feature that was moved (noted where so).
- **PC-only:** only one chat-scope call is refused over mobile access: `PUT /api/chat/permissions` (the default mode for new chats; `require_local_pc`, `src/gaia/ui/routers/chat.py`). Refusal text (403): "Change this on the PC running GAIA. It can't be changed over mobile access or from another computer." It is called from Settings > Permissions, outside this file list; it is listed as PRM-24 so the pairing is not lost. Two more PC-only refusals sit next to chat surfaces: the model-server log read behind the "Model server log" button (CV-31, 403: "Read GAIA's logs on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access or from another computer.") and provider-key entry (CMP-26, 403: "Enter provider keys on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access."). Nothing else in these 64 files is refused over the tunnel. But many actions "succeed" and act on the PC (open a file, show in folder, browse the PC's folders); those are called out in Notes, with PC-only left as No.
- Per-chat calls (`PUT /api/chat/permissions/{id}`, `POST /api/sessions/{id}/environment/...`, `POST /api/tunnel/start`) are not refused over mobile access.
- API prefix: every path is under `/api`. Streaming chat is SSE (`POST /api/chat/send`, `GET /api/chat/attach`).
- Keyboard-only affordances are listed because a phone needs a touch substitute. Where the web UI is hover-only, Notes says so.
Shared global shortcuts used by these surfaces (defined in App, outside this list): `Ctrl+K` (focus chat search), `Ctrl+Shift+O` (new chat), `Ctrl+B` (sidebar), `Ctrl+,` (Settings). On macOS the app labels them with the Command key.


### From settings.md

Reference: commit 7a6e05c2f (Agent UI 0.26.0). Owner runs 0.25.0 (tag v0.25.0).

Source: `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/components/`. Labels are quoted from `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/i18n/locales/en.ts` (English and Arabic only; `ar.ts` is the second locale).


### How to read this file (settings.md)

- **Since**: `0.25` if the feature exists at v0.25.0, `0.26` if added after. The i18n catalog (`en.ts`) did not exist at v0.25.0, so Since was decided from the v0.25.0 component source (hard-coded English labels) and its API calls. Where a feature existed at 0.25 under another label or place, Notes say so.
- **PC-only** is `Yes (Rn)` when the backend refuses the call over mobile access (the ngrok tunnel, `X-Forwarded-*` headers) or from another computer. The refusal codes are quoted once here:
| Code | Refusal text the user sees (HTTP 403) | Calls refused | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | "Change this on the PC running GAIA. It can't be changed over mobile access or from another computer." | `PUT /api/settings` when the body sets `network_policy`; `PUT /api/chat/permissions` (default mode); every `/api/messaging/*` call; `POST /api/gpu-memory/apply`; `POST /api/gpu-memory/revert`; `POST /api/setup/npu/memlock-fix` | `require_local_pc` in `security.py` |
| R2 | "Enter provider keys on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access." | `POST /api/providers/{id}/connect` when the body carries an API key (connecting with an already saved key is allowed) | `via_tunnel` in `routers/providers.py` |
| R3 | "Change the model server on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access." | `POST /api/lemonade/target` | `via_tunnel` in `routers/lemonade.py` |
| R4 | "Read GAIA's logs on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access or from another computer." | `GET /api/logs`, `GET /api/logs/{source}` | `_at_this_pc` in `routers/logs.py` |
- The web UI does not hide or grey out PC-only controls over the tunnel. The call fails and the component shows the refusal text inline (`settings-error` or `ErrorNotice`). A phone app should either hide those controls or show the refusal text.
- **Desktop shell only** means the control needs an Electron bridge: `window.gaiaAPI` (tray, taskbar flash, keep-awake), `window.gaiaUpdater` (updates), `window.gaiaInstall` (install screen). A phone has no equivalent, so these rows are "n/a on phone" unless the phone app chooses a substitute. No `window.gaiaDesktop` bridge exists in this commit.
- API paths are written with the `/api` prefix. Client functions live in `services/api.ts`, `setupApi.ts`, `memoryApi.ts`, `messagingApi.ts`, `speechApi.ts`, `chatTurnApi.ts`.
- Settings sections, in the order `SettingsDialog` shows them (`SECTIONS`): General, Setup, Model, Permissions, Skills, Connectors, Messaging, Memory, Notifications, Read aloud, Privacy, Advanced. That is 12. v0.25.0 had 8 (General, Model, Permissions, Skills, Connectors, Memory, Privacy, Advanced). Setup, Messaging, Notifications and Read aloud are 0.26.
Section ID codes: SET-NAV (dialog chrome), SET-GEN, SET-SETUP, SET-MODEL, SET-PERM, SET-SKILL, SET-CONN, SET-MSG, SET-MEM, SET-NOTIF, SET-VOICE, SET-PRIV, SET-ADV. FR = first-run setup and engine install screen. MS = model status, server, resources, logs, troubleshooting, updates, notices, error boundary.


### From library.md

Reference: commit 7a6e05c2f (Agent UI 0.26.0). Owner runs 0.25.0 (tag v0.25.0).

Labels are quoted from `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/i18n/locales/en.ts` (key in parentheses where it helps) or, where a string is hardcoded English in the component, from the component source (marked "hardcoded"). Electron-shell strings are quoted from the `.cjs` source.

How "Since" was decided: v0.25.0 has no i18n layer, no `pwa/`, no `manifest.json`, no `MemoryDashboardAboutYou`, no `IndexingProgress`, and none of the newer shell services (`tray-state`, `tray-host`, `keep-awake`, `window-frame`, `window-keys`, `quit-guard`, `folder-dialog`, `backend-supervisor`, the crash and renderer-gone dialogs, `engine-install`). So Since was judged per feature by reading the v0.25.0 component or service (`git archive v0.25.0`) and looking for the same control, not by key presence. A `0.25*` means the feature existed but its label or layout changed in 0.26 (the old label is noted).

Conventions: PC-only = "yes" only where the backend refuses a mobile-access request (see SEC). "n/a" for Electron-only behavior. API paths are as called by `services/*.ts` (`/api` prefix omitted by the client; written here with `/api`).

Phone-wide facts that affect many rows:

- A phone reaches the PC through an ngrok tunnel. Every `/api/*` and `/v1/*` call (except `GET /api/health`) must carry the tunnel token: `Authorization: Bearer <token>` or the `gaia_tunnel_token` cookie. The web UI gets the cookie from `https://<tunnel>/?token=<uuid>` (HttpOnly, SameSite=Strict); a native app cannot use that cookie bootstrap and must send the Bearer header. Missing or bad token returns 401 "Missing or invalid Authorization header" (`server.py` tunnel auth middleware).
- Every mutating request (non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS) to a guarded prefix needs header `X-Gaia-UI: 1`, and an `Origin`, if sent, must be loopback, the Host or the tunnel host (`UIRequestGuardMiddleware`). A native app sends no Origin (allowed) but must send `X-Gaia-UI: 1`. A wrong Host header is a 400 "Invalid Host header: ...".
- Memory discovery/inference progress, connector events, session events and chat streams use `EventSource` (SSE). React Native has no built-in EventSource; it needs a polyfill that can set the Bearer header.
- The web app already has a mobile layout (`max-width: 768px`): sidebar becomes an overlay, the Mobile access button is hidden, and each chat row gets a single "⋯" menu.

## Decisions

Rich's answers, relayed by the Lead on 2026-11-12 (he asked Rich directly).

1. **First-run setup on the phone: in scope.** The phone runs full setup, so the 71 setup rows (`FR-*` except the desktop engine installer) are targets.
2. **File actions that open things on the PC: different on purpose.** On the phone, Open, Show in folder and file-path links preview or share the file, and never open anything on the PC (CV-26, CV-46, SP-06, SP-24, DOC-18).
3. **Unattended work keeps running while the phone listener is on**, and approvals made from a phone are logged per device (the T1 security review, `2026-11-12-t1-security-review.md`). The schedule rows (SCH) need no pause state for the phone.
4. **A Google account connects on the PC only** (a known limit: the callback is on the PC's loopback, and only the Microsoft connector supports the device-code flow). RC-09, EML-01 and CON-07 carry the note.
5. **The 56 desktop-shell exclusions are approved.**

## Rich cards in answers (RC)

Cards arrive as `tool_result.render` with `data`; the registry maps these keys: `email_pre_scan`, `table`, `key_value`, `list`, `image`, `audio`, `diff`, `needs_confirmation`, `needs_input`, `connect_account`, `guide`, `calculator`. Unknown keys and invalid payloads show a fallback card; a thrown error shows a boundary message.

| ID | Feature | Status | Since | PC-only | Evidence | Exact label(s) | States | API | Component | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| RC-01 | Table card | missing | 0.25 | No | code | optional title, header row, rows; overflow "+{n} more (truncated)" | rows or columns capped at 500 |  | TableCard.tsx | Wide tables need horizontal scroll. |
| RC-02 | Key-value card | missing | 0.25 | No | code | optional title, key and value rows; "+{n} more (truncated)" | capped at 500 |  | KeyValueCard.tsx |  |
| RC-03 | List card | missing | 0.25 | No | code | optional title, ordered or bullet list; "+{n} more (truncated)" | capped at 500 |  | ListCard.tsx |  |

## Session panel (SP)

The panel is one docked column per chat, or an overlay sheet at 1099 px wide or narrower. Sections read when they open, when expanded and when an answer finishes; a closed panel or collapsed section reads nothing (except the Files recheck, SP-05).

| ID | Feature | Status | Since | PC-only | Evidence | Exact label(s) | States | API | Component | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SP-01 | Panel shell | missing | 0.25.4 | No | code | landmark "Session panel" (`sp.label`); heading "This chat" (`sp.title`); close button aria "Close session panel", title "Close" (`sp.close`, `sp.closeShort`) | docked or overlay |  | SessionPanel.tsx | Overlay: backdrop tap closes it, Escape closes, focus is trapped and moves to the close button. Open state, width and the expanded sections are remembered (localStorage `gaia.sessionPanel.*`), separately for overlay and docked. |
| SP-02 | Resize edge | missing | 0.25.4 | No | code | separator aria "Resize session panel" (`sp.resize`) | dragging |  | SessionPanel.tsx | Docked only: 280 to 640 px, capped at half the window, double-click resets, Arrow, Home and End keys, flips in right-to-left. No touch use. |
| SP-03 | Collapsible section header | missing | 0.26 | No | code | section title and an optional one-line summary shown when collapsed | expanded, collapsed |  | PanelSection.tsx | The body mounts only while open, so a collapsed section fetches nothing. A request to reveal a section (from the context meter) scrolls it into view. |
| SP-04 | This answer: in-progress and error states | missing | 0.25.4 | No | code | "Working on this answer. Its sources, files and where its data went appear here when it finishes." (`rail.pending`); error "Couldn't put together this turn's sources and details: {error}" (`rail.error`) | pending, error |  | SideRail.tsx | The section appears only when the chat has an answer; section title "This answer" (`sp.section.answer`). |

Scripts, programs and web pages offer only Show in folder (`openable` false). Open is refused for anything but documents, data and images; paths must be under the PC user's home folder. Note this section does poll (every 4 s) in spite of the guide's "never checks on a timer".; Different on purpose (Rich, via the Lead, 2026-11-12): on the phone, preview or share the file; never open it on the PC |

## Settings: Messaging (SET-MSG)

The whole `/api/messaging` router requires the PC (R1), including the status read. A phone connected through the tunnel will get R1 on every call, so the pane cannot work on a phone.

## First-run setup and engine install (FR)

First-run setup is `SetupScreen` (also reopened from Settings → Setup → "Run setup again" and from "Set up local models"). Its steps in the rail are "Check this PC", "Your setup", "Download", "Test", "Ready"; "Customize" adds sub-screens (GPU memory, models, cloud key). At 0.25 it was a single screen: choose where GAIA runs (On this PC, Fireworks AI, AMD LLM Gateway), enter a key, optionally pick an existing Lemonade, then a progress view. Everything else below is 0.26 unless marked.
Re-run mode shows "Back to GAIA" and Settings is closed first. The `POST /api/setup/run`, `/api/setup/models/*`, `/api/setup/cancel` and status calls are not refused over the tunnel by the backend, but the work (install, downloads, model tests) runs on the PC. Only the GPU memory change and the NPU memlock fix are PC-only (R1).

## SCH: Scheduled tasks

Opened from the sidebar "Scheduled tasks" (`sidebar.schedules`) as a dialog over the chat. `ScheduleManager.tsx`. `App.tsx` also polls `GET /api/schedules` every 30 s so a failed run raises a notification with the panel closed (APP-13).

| ID | Feature | Status | Since | PC-only | Evidence | Exact label(s) | States | API | Component | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

(`schedules.loading`); load failure "Failed to load schedules" (`schedules.loadFailed`); empty "No scheduled tasks yet. Create one to get started." (`schedules.empty`) | `GET /api/schedules` | ScheduleManager |  |
| SCH-02 | Dismissible action error | missing | 0.25.4 | no | code | aria "Dismiss error" (`schedules.dismissError`) |  | from store `actionError` | ScheduleManager |  |

(`schedules.whenHint`); "Parsing..." (`schedules.parsing`); preview "Next run: {when}" (`schedules.nextRun`); rejection "GAIA didn't understand that schedule." ## EML: Email connect and pre-scan

Both render inside chat messages, not as screens. Their strings are hardcoded English (not in en.ts).

| ID | Feature | Status | Since | PC-only | Evidence | Exact label(s) | States | API | Component | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| EML-01 | Connect-account card shown when a reply says an account is not connected | missing | 0.25 | no | code | region "Connect email account"; text "Connect your email account to use Email Triage."; after a click "A browser tab opened for sign-in. Return here when finished."; buttons "Connect Google", "Connect Microsoft", "Connect Microsoft 365" (aria `Connect {label}`); after click "Reopen {label} sign-in" | idle, busy spinner, done (reopen), error text (role=alert) | `GET /api/connectors/{id}`, `POST /api/connectors/{id}/authorize` (scopes, grant_agents = the email agent), then opens `authorization_url` | `email/EmailConnectCta.tsx`, rendered by `MessageBubble.tsx` | Triggered by detecting `NOT_CONNECTED:`, `AGENT_NOT_GRANTED:`, `AUTH_REQUIRED:` or "connectors → google" text in the reply. Browser OAuth lands on the PC's loopback callback, so it cannot complete from a phone browser; offer the device-code flow (CON-12) or send the user to the PC. Opens via `window.gaia.openExternal` or `window.open`.; Known limit (Rich, via the Lead, 2026-11-12): a Google account connects on the PC only; Microsoft can use the device-code flow from the phone |
| EML-02 | "Connected since this reply" retry banner | missing | 0.25 (re-built in 0.26: Connect card providers, #7164) | no | code | "{label} connected since this reply — ask again to run it now." ; button "Ask again"; dismiss "×" (aria "Dismiss") (all hardcoded) | hidden until the referenced provider shows configured; dismissed | `GET /api/connectors` on mount, on window focus, and every 4000 ms until connected | `ConnectorRetryBanner.tsx` (shown above the composer in `ChatView`) | Refresh on app foreground (replaces window focus). Re-runs the last question via `handleRetryLast`. |

## Appendix A: routes the backend refuses over mobile access

Reference, not parity rows: each belongs to a feature row above. Verified against every `require_local_pc` and `via_tunnel` use in main's backend (8 guard sites, 15 routes).

| ID | Route | UI surface | Refusal message | Since | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SEC-01 | `GET /api/logs` | Logs dialog (`LogsDialog.tsx`; opened from Settings → Advanced "View logs", the model status pill "Model server log", and a chat message error link) | HTTP 403 "Read GAIA's logs on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access or from another computer." | 0.26 | Also requires `X-Gaia-UI: 1`. Route is new in 0.26 (router `logs.py` is not in v0.25.0). |
| SEC-02 | `GET /api/logs/{source}` | same dialog (sources "Model server", "GAIA", "Background service", "Agent", "Desktop app", "Other"); "Save all logs" in Settings → Advanced | same as SEC-01 | 0.26 | `model_server` source reads Lemonade's live log. |
| SEC-03 | `POST /api/gpu-memory/apply` | Settings GPU memory panel (`setup/GpuMemoryPanel.tsx`, "GPU memory", button "Apply") | HTTP 403 "Change this on the PC running GAIA. It can't be changed over mobile access or from another computer." | 0.26 | Starts a root step on the PC. |
| SEC-04 | `POST /api/gpu-memory/revert` | same panel, confirm "Undo the GPU memory change?" | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 | `GET /api/gpu-memory`, `POST` and `DELETE /api/gpu-memory/pending` are not refused (UI header only). |
| SEC-05 | `POST /api/setup/npu/memlock-fix` | Setup screen, NPU not ready (`setup/NpuNotReady.tsx`) | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 | Raises the locked-memory limit with an administrator prompt (pkexec) on the PC. |
| SEC-06 | `GET /api/messaging/discord` | Settings → Messaging (Discord) | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 | Router-level guard: every route under `/api/messaging` is refused, reads included, because the status carries bot and pairing links. Router and Discord bridge are new in 0.26. |
| SEC-07 | `POST /api/messaging/discord/connect` | Settings → Messaging, bot token entry | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 | A phone must not be able to swap in its own bot. |
| SEC-08 | `POST /api/messaging/discord/start` | Settings → Messaging | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 |  |
| SEC-09 | `POST /api/messaging/discord/stop` | Settings → Messaging | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 |  |
| SEC-10 | `POST /api/messaging/discord/pair` | Settings → Messaging | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 |  |
| SEC-11 | `POST /api/messaging/discord/disconnect` | Settings → Messaging | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 |  |
| SEC-12 | `PUT /api/chat/permissions` | Settings → Permissions, the default permission mode (`PermissionsSettings.tsx`; `api.setDefaultPermissionMode`) | same as SEC-03 | 0.26 | Sets `full_access` in `~/.gaia/config.json` for every future chat. Per-chat `PUT /api/chat/permissions/{sessionId}` and the permission chip in a chat are not refused. |
| SEC-13 | `PUT /api/settings` only when the body sets `network_policy` | Settings → Privacy, "Local only" switch (`PrivacySettings.tsx`) | same as SEC-03 (raised by `require_local_pc` inside `update_settings`) | 0.26 | Other fields of the same route (`custom_model`, `context_size`, `agent_mode`, `dynamic_tools`) are allowed from a phone. Reading `GET /api/settings` is allowed. |
| SEC-14 | `POST /api/lemonade/target` | Settings → Model, model server section ("Use this server" / "Use GAIA’s own", `LemonadeServerSection.tsx`), the setup screen and `ModelServerDownNotice` | HTTP 403 "Change the model server on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access." | 0.25 | Present in v0.25.0 (`_via_tunnel` in `routers/lemonade.py`). |
| SEC-15 | `POST /api/providers/{provider}/connect` only when the body carries a non-empty `api_key` | Settings → Model, cloud provider "API key" field ("Connect") and the setup screen | HTTP 403 "Enter provider keys on the PC running GAIA, not over mobile access." | 0.25 | Present in v0.25.0. Connecting with a blank key (to reuse a saved key) is allowed over the tunnel. |

## Appendix B: inventory notes

### Files with no row (chat.md)


| File | Why |
|---|---|
| MarkedText.tsx | Helper that wraps text with direction marks (`markLtr`, `<MarkedText>`) so numbers and names don't reverse in right-to-left text; it renders nothing of its own. Used by permission prompts, the model menu, answer stats and the comparison. |
| Popover.tsx | Layout container for every anchored menu in this list (model menu, permission chip, context meter, spend meter, retry menu, row "⋯" menu). Behavior only: flips above or below, caps its height, focuses the first button, closes on outside press or Escape. It has no label of its own. On a phone these become bottom sheets. |
| InfoBubble.tsx | Hover or focus info icon used only inside the model menu (see CMP-23); it has no feature of its own beyond the "About {name}" aria label. Hover-only; replace with a tap. |
| render/registry.tsx | Maps `render` keys to components (see the section 5 header); no UI. |
| render/RenderCard.tsx | Wrapper that resolves the registry and adds the error boundary; no UI of its own. |
| ExportErrorBar.tsx | Rendered only as a dismissible error bar; its two uses are CV-05 and CV-12. |
| session-panel/Switch.tsx | Listed as SP-30 for completeness; it is a pure control. |
| UnsupportedFeature.tsx | Only its `bugReportUrl` helper is used by the chat (CV-30). The `UploadErrorToast`, the unsupported-file-type table (images, video, audio, archive, ...), `featureRequestUrl` and `PERMANENT_FAILURE_HINTS` it exports belong to the document library and file browser (other lane). |
| StartCards.tsx | Has a row (NC-14), but note it is not rendered on the New chat screen in 0.26. |

### Open questions (chat.md)


1. **Message-actions hover on a phone.** The web UI relies on hover (`.msg:hover .msg-actions`) for every message except the latest answer. The native app needs a decision (long-press sheet versus an always-visible row). The matrix should treat CV-16 to CV-23 as one "message actions" feature with a touch alternative.
2. **Session-panel Files polling.** The guide says the panel "never checks on a timer", but `SideRail.tsx` re-checks file status every 4 s (`FILE_RECHECK_MS`) while the section is visible. I treated the code as authoritative; the guide may be stale (not edited, as this task is read-only).
3. **Open / Show in folder from a phone.** The backend does not refuse `POST /api/files/open` over the tunnel; it launches Explorer or Finder on the PC. I marked these rows PC-only = No and described the behavior in Notes. If the parity matrix wants "PC-only" to include "acts on the PC with no effect on the phone", CV-26, CV-46, SP-06 and SP-24 are the rows to flip.
4. **Log button over the tunnel.** Resolved: the refusal text is quoted in CV-31. Left open: whether the web UI hides or shows the 403 when a phone taps the button (the component shows it for any local-model failure, so a phone user would see the error).
5. **"Since" for restructured features.** `en.ts` does not exist at v0.25.0 (strings were inline), so Since follows the code. Where a 0.25 feature was rebuilt (the answer stats line CV-38, the agent activity block CV-41, the model menu CMP-23, the permission prompt PRM-05 and PRM-09) I marked the feature 0.25 or 0.26 per the rows' Notes; a stricter reading could split them.
6. **Strings not in en.ts.** Hard-coded English remains in TurnChanges, ChangesReview, ChangesSection (Commit / PR dialogs and result messages), QueuedMessages, SessionDocsBar, PrivateChatButton, ChatView (a number of titles), GuideCard notices and the calculator labels, ConfirmationCard, UnsupportedCard and CardErrorBoundary, the ModelChip download-error messages and RetryWithMenu empty states. A phone app measured against the web UI's localization will need these keys added (or left English).
7. **Server-supplied text.** The "older messages out of view" note (CV-52), library notices (CV-53), the guide's "75 minutes without progress" reply (CV-40), start-screen card titles (NC-14), the model's place labels and cloud privacy notice (CMP-22, CMP-24) arrive as English from the server; I could not determine whether the Arabic build translates them (some are rebuilt from the English via `cardLine`).
8. **EmailPreScanCard and ConnectorRetryBanner / ModelServerDownNotice / TroubleshootingReport / ResourceMonitor / DownloadChip / ModelStatusPill** are mounted by the chat surfaces but sit outside this file list; I gave each a pointer row (CV-02, CV-13, CV-14, CV-31, SB-25, SB-26, RC-20) and did not inventory them.

### Files with no row (settings.md)


| File | Why |
|---|---|
| settings/SettingsDisclosure.tsx | A reusable collapsible `<details>` wrapper (title, count, hint, force-open); it renders no content of its own. Its behavior (opens by default, forced open when a search matches or an error needs attention) is cited in the rows that use it. |
| settings/ErrorNotice.tsx | A shared error block ("GAIA can't reach its model server" / "Models on this PC aren't set up yet", "Check again", "Technical details", "Set up local models"). Covered by SET-ADV-03, SET-MODEL-09, SET-MODEL-20; it has no screen of its own. |
| setup/Reveal.tsx | Animation wrapper that expands or collapses a block; no content. |
| setup/FollowLog.tsx | A scrolling monospace log box that sticks to the newest line; its content comes from StageLogs and DownloadProgress (FR-LOG-01, FR-DL-04). |
| setup/SetupFooter.tsx | A portal slot for the step's button bar; layout only (FR-08). |
| setup/ConfirmSheet.tsx | Rows FR-CONFIRM-01 and FR-CONFIRM-02 cover the sheet; the file is the generic modal. |
| setup/LanguagePicker.tsx | Covered by FR-04; it is one `<select>`. |
| setup/DownloadContents.tsx, setup/RunActivity.tsx, setup/StageLogs.tsx, setup/DownloadChip.tsx | Each is covered by the named rows (FR-PLAN-06, FR-CHECK-04 and FR-DL-02, FR-LOG-01, FR-DL-11); listed here to confirm they were read. |
| NpuBackdrop.tsx | Decorative only; MS-NPU-01 records it so the parity matrix can mark "skip". |
| SetupSteps.tsx | Not a screen; it holds the Models step layout and the Ready summary (FR-MODELS-08 to FR-MODELS-13). |

### Open questions (settings.md)


1. **`ConnectorsSection` is not in this file list.** `ConnectorsPane.tsx` only wraps it. Its controls (Google, Microsoft, GitHub OAuth, MCP-server connectors, per-agent grants) need rows from whoever owns it. The server also treats `/v1/connections` as sensitive over the tunnel (`server.py` gate), but I did not find a per-call `require_local_pc` on the connector routers in `src/gaia/ui/`; that was not checked in depth because the routers live outside the stated scope.
2. **Shortcut list at 0.25 (SET-GEN-20).** The 0.25 source has Send, New line, Stop, New chat, Search chats, Show or hide the sidebar and Settings by label; the exact key chords at 0.25 were not compared.
3. **0.25 label evidence is by source search, not by a running 0.25 app.** Since values for features that existed at 0.25 under different wording (for example update controls in Advanced, the Device select in Model, `Context size in tokens` in Advanced) come from reading `git show v0.25.0:` sources. Where a 0.25 label differs, Notes say so.
4. **Platforms for GPU memory.** `gpu_memory.status()` returns `applies: false` and hides the step on non-AMD-iGPU machines, but I did not confirm whether macOS or Windows-on-Intel are both hidden by `_hidden` or only by the AMD check. For a phone this affects only what the PC reports.
5. **Resource strip and the memory dashboard are PC data.** I could not confirm whether `/api/system/resources` is reachable over the tunnel with the tunnel token (it is not gated by `require_local_pc`), so it may work from a phone, but nothing in the web UI is mobile-aware.
6. **Settings values stored on the device versus the PC.** Appearance, colors, language, resource-strip visibility and all Notifications settings are client-side (`chatStore`, `resourceMonitorStore`, `notificationStore`), so a phone would hold its own copy; nothing syncs them. The guide says language is "remembered by this device".
7. **The guide's Models table row for Local says "pick CPU, GPU or NPU for new chats".** The Device control that did this was removed in 0.26 (no row exists). The guide is stale on that point.
8. **Phone reachability of first-run setup.** The setup calls are not refused over the tunnel, but the UI gate (`/api/setup/check`) normally runs before a phone could pair. Nothing in the code says a phone may run setup; treat FR as PC-first unless Rich decides otherwise.

### Files with no row (library.md)


| File | Why |
|---|---|
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/components/ConnectorTileMenu.tsx` | Has rows (CON-06); listed here only to confirm it was covered. |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/services/index-query.cjs` | Builds the `file://` query string the window loads (backend port); no UI. |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/services/process-tree.cjs`, `lock-holders.cjs`, `quit-cleanup.cjs`, `backend-orphan.cjs`, `port-manager.cjs` | Process and port plumbing; user-visible only through the dialogs already in SHELL-25, SHELL-27, SHELL-30, SHELL-31. |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/services/tray-icons.cjs`, `tray-host.cjs`, `app-icon.cjs` | Icon drawing and platform checks; visible effects are in SHELL-01 and SHELL-11. |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/services/window-reveal.cjs` | Shows the window once painted (avoids a flash); no UI. |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/services/agent-seeder.cjs`, `agent-process-manager.cjs` | Background services; only their crash notifications are visible (SHELL-20, SHELL-32). |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/bin/gaia-ui.cjs`, `scripts/*.cjs` | npm CLI entry and packaging scripts; not part of the running UI. |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/pwa/serviceWorkerPlugin.ts` | Vite build plugin that stamps the cache name; folded into MOB-21. |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/pwa/register.ts` | Registers `sw.js`; no UI (MOB-21). |
| `src/gaia/apps/webui/src/hooks/useTrayBridge.ts`, `src/utils/mobileAccessEvents.ts` | Glue between the window and the shell (SHELL-01..09, MOB-11), not read directly; they were read to document those rows. |

### Open questions (library.md)


1. Push: no server-side push or relay exists in the web UI. NTF-17 shows browser Notification only while the page is open. Whether the backend can emit push for approvals, questions and finished chats (needed to replace OS notifications) was not found.
2. Several visible strings in my files are hardcoded English and absent from `en.ts`: the whole of `EmailConnectCta`, `EmailPreScanCard`, `ConnectorRetryBanner`, parts of `MobileAccessModal` ("Tunnel active", "Mobile URL", "Stop tunnel", QR fallbacks, the instructions list), most of the ConnectionBanner download/context/error copy, the policy toast "Blocked: {tool} is restricted by policy.", and the prompts sent by FileBrowser ("Please analyze this document for me: ..."). The parity matrix should either quote these from source (as done here) or treat them as untranslated.
3. CON-13 Since: 0.25 had a "Check" button in `ConnectorsSection` (16 hits for device/Check terms) but I did not diff the slow/failed copy; the row says 0.26 for the copy only.
4. Settings panels (Notifications, Desktop app, Updates, Model, Privacy, Permissions, Messaging, Advanced) are owned by another inventory; their controls appear here only where they drive a shell or PC-only behavior (NTF-16, SHELL-13..16, SHELL-23, SEC rows).
5. Whether a native app can pass the ngrok interstitial ("Tunnel password" hint, MOB-07) without a browser: not determined; depends on the ngrok plan and headers.
6. `POST /api/files/open` and the browse/search routes are not guarded over the tunnel. It is undetermined whether that is intended (a phone can list the PC's directories and open files there). Worth confirming before the phone app exposes DOC-18 and DOC-30..40.
7. Whether SEC-A2 (autonomous loop idles during a tunnel) has any user-facing text: none found in `en.ts`.


## Changelog

- v1 (2026-11-12): built from the inventory at `7a6e05c2f`: 669 rows, 613 targets, every target `missing` or `decide`.
- v2 (2026-11-12): Rich's decisions, via the Lead. Setup is in scope (71 rows `decide` → `missing`); the five PC-opening file actions are `different`; the Google limit is noted on three rows; the n/a rows are approved.
